### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 澳洲 8 月就業人數勁增 3.95 萬人大幅優於預期，但勞動參與率上升至 67.1%，
推升失業率至 4.6%，呈現勞動力擴增但市場邊際降溫跡象。澳洲央行總裁布拉
克日前指出，失業率回升至5%有助於抑制頑固通膨。考量物價長期高於目標中
點，市場預期澳洲央行本週二將重啟緊縮，升息 1 碼至 4.60%，創 2011年底以
來新高。
 瑞士央行（SNB）上週四宣布連續第五次將關鍵政策利率維持在0%不變，符合
市場預期。儘管受油價推升使 8 月通膨微升至 0.8%，但仍處於目標區間，央行
預期中期通膨壓力溫和，現行極度寬鬆立場依然適當。決議公布後瑞郎兌美元跌
至 1.2077。相較全球主要央行相繼緊縮，瑞士央行維持超低利率，策略轉為視情
況調節匯市以穩定瑞郎。
 標普全球公布歐元區 9 月綜合 PMI 初值升至 53.1，創三年新高，製造與服務業
同步擴張，新訂單增速達四年多最快，印證經濟無懼美伊衝突引發的能源成本飆

升，展現超預期韌性。受能源價格傳導加速影響，通膨壓力再度升溫，儘管尚未
浮現薪資二輪效應，但強勁景氣數據促使市場大幅提高歐洲央行提前於10月例
會再度升息的押注。
 美國 9 月綜合 PMI 初值勁升至 58.4，創 62 個月新高；製造業與服務業 PMI 亦
逆勢走強至 57與 58.7，凸顯企業擴張強勁，引發市場對通膨持續性的擔憂。市
場焦點轉向 9 月 30日公布的美國 8 月 PCE、個人消費支出與 Q2 GDP終值，同
日日本亦將發布短觀調查與央行意見摘要。若終端消費延續強勁，將牽動美日央
行政策利率預期。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.655，低點在 31.866，以 31.790作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 2.8分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，台股創新高，外資連續五日買超台股並匯入，推升
台幣最高來到 31.655，不過逢低吸引進口商、政府基金等買盤進場，台幣主要在
31.700 附近震盪；臨近周末，美國 9 月綜合 PMI 由 8 月的 56 走升至 58.4，創下逾
5 年來的新高，加上美伊戰事持續，皆推升市場對後續升息的預期，美元指數升至
101 上方，台幣跳空開低，回貶至 31.800 上方，最低來到 31.866，不過隨後出口商
賣壓出籠，台幣跌勢放緩，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.790。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國經濟表現仍具韌性，9月綜合
PMI 由 8 月的 56 上升至 58.4，企業活動加速至逾 5 年來最強，數據公布後美國 10
年債券殖利率升破 5%，Fed Watch 顯示市場對於聯準會 10 月升息預期升高，有助
於美元指數維持偏強格局，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，台股在創下
新高後回落，而外資也結束連續五日買超的走勢，轉為賣超，短線上資金匯入不利
台幣表現，然而，台灣8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6億美元、年增 71.4%，創歷史新高，
AI 需求帶動出口持續強勁，出口商後續拋匯需求預計將提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，
預估本週台幣區間震盪。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走貶。人民幣中間價在上周連續十天走升，並升破6.7500整數
關口，最高來到 6.7468，帶動人民幣偏升，不過由於美伊戰事持續，市場對於聯準
會升息預期提升，美元指數震盪走強，加上週間公布美國9月綜合 PMI由 8 月的 56
走升至 58.4，創下逾 5 年來的新高，美元指數突破 101 整數關卡，令境外人民幣承
壓，USD/CNH回貶至 6.7200上方，終場境外人民幣收在6.7227，週線貶值283點，
境內人民幣則收在 6.7125，週線貶值 141 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣高檔震盪。就國際市場來看，美中兩國完成新一輪談判，美方
表示雙方同意將原訂 11 月 10 日到期的貿易休戰延長至明年 1 月 10 日，並持續就
關稅、貿易及供應鏈等議題進行協商，降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險，對人民幣形
成一定支撐。不過，美國近期經濟數據仍具韌性，加以聯準會偏鷹立場推升美元，
預計將限制人民幣升值空間；就中國國內市場來看，近期人行持續偏強設定中間價，
帶動人民幣維持偏強走勢，惟美中利差再度擴大，預計將限制人民幣升值的空間；
最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，匯價反彈至 20日均線之上，顯示原先下
行動能暫歇，上方壓力先看向季線 6.7350附近，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣高檔震
盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6900-6.7400

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY先上後下。週初，美國多位聯準會官員鷹派談話，帶動美元指數
持續上行，推升 USD/JPY延續前一週的走勢來到 157上方；週間，受美國 PMI 強
於預期與美債殖利率全面上行帶動，市場重新提高對聯準會後續升息路徑的押注，
帶動 USD/JPY大漲突破 159的關卡；臨近週末，日本首相高市早苗表態日圓被低估
是個問題，使市場提高對政策訊號與潛在干預的敏感度，帶動USD/JPY回吐大部分
漲幅，終場收在 157.26，週線上漲 56點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本財務大臣片山皋月上週
五罕見透露，美國總統川普在美日元首峰會上對日圓過度疲弱表達關切，首相高市
早苗亦回應日圓遭低估確屬問題。儘管近期美元指數維持在 101 上方，但在日本政
府可能干預的陰影下，預計將限縮日圓貶值的空間。二、美國9 月綜合 PMI產出指
數創逾五年新高，疊加美伊戰事推升油價突破100美元與柴油大漲，通膨壓力劇增。
美聯儲理事巴爾表態可能需再升息兩次，芝加哥聯儲古爾斯比也示警能源衝擊具持
續性，推升市場押注 10 月升息機率升至六成，預計將支撐 USD/JPY 走勢。三、就
技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY連日上漲一度站回年線但隨即失守，目前依舊承
壓於季線及年線下方，不過各天期均線趨平，且下方尚有月線支撐，短線尚無明確
的方向。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：155.30-159.30 
前週交易區間 
156.56 159.03

### Page 6

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，歐元延續前一週偏弱的走勢，加上聯準會官員鷹
派發言，導致歐元跌破 1.1450的重要關卡；週間，儘管油價自高檔回落，但市場解
讀為能源成本仍處高位、難以迅速回落，推升對美國實質利率與利差優勢的預期。
加上歐洲在能源依賴與成長動能較弱下，相對於美國更難承受長期高利率環境，歐
元最低來到 1.1358；臨近週末，歐元跌深反彈，終場收在1.1391，週線下跌 91點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行首席經濟學家連
恩示警，第二波能源價格飆升將推升通膨，預計至2027年中期始回落至目標水準。
德國央行月報指出，受中東衝突、低天然氣庫存及明年醫療改革影響，通膨將居高
不下；夏季雖因出口疲弱與乾旱致增長趨緩，但在製造業回暖與財政支出挹注下，
估年底經濟有望重啟復甦，預計將提供歐元一定的支撐。二、美聯儲官員接連釋放
強烈鷹派信號，聖路易聯儲總裁穆薩萊姆直言當前 3.75%-4.00%利率仍偏寬鬆，呼
籲盡早收緊；芝加哥聯儲總裁古爾斯比亦警告，AI投資繁榮引發需求過熱，疊加持
續性供應衝擊，可能迫使美聯儲加速且更積極地升息。目前Fedwatch顯示預期明年
4 月前可能再升息三次，將不利歐元表現。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
在各天期均線下方，且接連失守1.15及 1.14整數關卡，空方動能較佔優勢。綜合以
上，預估 EUR/USD本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1300-1.1450 
前週交易區間 
1.1358 1.1495

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.09.21-115.09.24 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.804% ~ 0.830%  
30天期短票利率   1.65% ~ 1.69%  
公債－10 年期-A15109     1.95%  
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.84%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，本周央行持續每日減發定存單，令市場資金充裕，帶動短
率靠向區間低位。 
週二央行標售 2 年期定存單 250 億元，加權平均得標利率為 1.638%，略低於
市場預期，且較上次標售結果下跌 2.5個基本點。 
週四隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.804%~0.830%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.65%~1.69%，利率較上週下跌 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109 及 5 年期指標券 A15108 均未成
交，週四等殖系統參考報價中價分別為 1.95%、1.84%。 
台股排擠效應、國際債市走勢偏空，加上油價續處高檔，通膨潛在威脅仍存，
12 月升息可能性未能完全排除，市場氣氛續偏觀望，預期台債殖利率將維持
高檔區間盤整 ， 預估5年券區間為 1.84%~1.90%，10年券區間為 1.95%~2.0%。

    固定资产投资:月累计同比(%) 0.27 -7.47 -7.20 -7.20 -6.70 -5.70 -4.10 -1.60 1.70 1.80 -- -3.80 -2.60 -1.70
     工业：工业生产较快增长，装备制造业和高技术制造业支撑明显。前三季度，全国规模以上工业增加值同比增长5.3%。分三大门类看，采矿业增加值同比增长2.0%，制造业增长5.7%，电力、热力、燃气及水生产和供应业增长5.4%。装备制造业增加值同比增长
10.2%，高技术制造业增加值增长14.3%，分别快于全部规模以上工业增加值4.9和9.0个百分点。分经济类型看，国有控股企业增加值同比增长3.8%；股份制企业增长5.8%，外商及港澳台投资企业增长3.2%；私营企业增长4.3%。分产品看，3D打印设备、锂离子电池、工业机
器人产品产量同比分别增长62.5%、39.5%、29.1%。9月份，规模以上工业增加值同比增长5.5%，比上月加快0.3个百分点；环比增长0.61%。9月份，制造业采购经理指数为50.1%，比上月上升0.3个百分点；企业生产经营活动预期指数为53.8%，与上月持平。1—8月份，全国
规模以上工业企业实现利润总额52720亿元，同比增长15.7%。
     服务：服务业平稳增长，现代服务业增势良好。前三季度，服务业增加值同比增长5.2%。其中，租赁和商务服务业，信息传输、软件和信息技术服务业，金融业，住宿和餐饮业增加值分别增长12.3%、10.4%、5.8%、5.5%。9月份，全国服务业生产指数同比增长4.8%，
比上月加快0.7个百分点。其中，信息传输、软件和信息技术服务业，租赁和商务服务业，交通运输、仓储和邮政业，住宿和餐饮业生产指数分别增长9.8%、9.5%、5.4%、5.3%。9月份，服务业商务活动指数为50.2%，比上月上升0.9个百分点；服务业业务活动预期指数为
55.5%，与上月持平。其中，电信广播电视及卫星传输服务、货币金融服务、保险等行业商务活动指数位于55.0%以上较高景气区间。1—8月份，规模以上服务业企业营业收入同比增长6.0%。
     消费：消费市场继续扩容，服务零售和网上零售较快增长。前三季度，社会消费商品和服务零售总额同比增长2.6%，其中服务零售额增长5.0%，商品零售额增长1.0%。在服务零售额中，通讯信息服务类、旅游咨询租赁服务类、文体休闲服务类零售额较快增长。前三
季度，社会消费品零售总额370001亿元，同比增长1.1%。按经营单位所在地分，城镇消费品零售额319820亿元，同比增长0.9%；乡村消费品零售额50181亿元，增长2.3%。按消费类型分，商品零售额328032亿元，同比增长1.0%；餐饮收入41969亿元，增长2.4%。基本生活类
和部分升级类商品销售较快增长，限额以上单位粮油食品类、通讯器材类、文化办公用品类商品零售额同比分别增长6.8%、18.1%、6.2%。前三季度，全国网上商品和服务零售额151471亿元，同比增长4.4%。其中，网上商品零售额95060亿元，增长3.8%；网上服务零售额
56411亿元，增长5.3%。9月份，社会消费品零售总额42433亿元，同比增长1.1%，比上月加快0.7个百分点；环比增长0.35%。
     投资：固定资产投资下降，知识产权产品投资较快增长。前三季度，全国固定资产投资（不含农户）334115亿元，同比下降7.2%，与1—8月份持平；扣除房地产开发的固定资产投资下降4.2%。其中，知识产权产品投资同比增长9.3%。高技术产业投资同比增长4.9%，
其中信息服务业，航空、航天器及设备制造业，电子及通信设备制造业投资分别增长20.2%、12.5%、7.6%。分产业看，第一产业投资同比下降3.9%，第二产业投资下降2.7%，第三产业投资下降10.0%。分领域看，基础设施投资同比下降4.2%，制造业投资下降2.1%，房地产
开发投资下降20.6%。民间投资同比下降10.4%；扣除房地产开发的民间投资下降6.7%。全国新建商品房销售面积56483万平方米，同比下降13.2%；新建商品房销售额53799亿元，下降13.9%。全国二手房交易网签面积61746万平方米，同比增长10.7%。9月份，固定资产投资
（不含农户）环比增长0.07%。
    进出口：货物进出口快速增长，贸易结构继续优化。前三季度，货物进出口总额399399亿元，同比增长18.8%。其中，出口231979亿元，增长16.4%；进口167420亿元，增长22.3%。对共建“一带一路”国家进出口增长17.1%。民营企业进出口增长19.1%，占进出口总额
的比重为56.9%。机电产品出口增长23.7%，占出口总额的比重为64.3%。9月份，进出口总额51628亿元，同比增长27.5%，比上月加快7.7个百分点。其中，出口30344亿元，增长29.6%；进口21284亿元，增长24.6%。
近一年变动趋势
2026年前三季度与9月宏观经济数据
冠通期货研究报告
宏观数据

国家统计局10月19日发布2026年前三季度与9月宏观经济数据。初步核算，前三季度国内生产总值1071071亿元，按不变价格计算，同比增长4.6%。分产业看，第一产业增加值58994亿元，同比增长3.7%；第二产业
增加值383822亿元，增长3.8%；第三产业增加值628255亿元，增长5.2%。分季度看，一季度国内生产总值同比增长5.0%，二季度增长4.3%，三季度增长4.5%。从环比看，三季度国内生产总值增长1.2%。

物价：居民消费价格温和上涨，工业生产者价格涨幅扩大。前三季度，全国居民消费价格（CPI）同比上涨0.9%。分类别看，食品烟酒及在外餐饮价格同比下降0.4%，衣着价格上涨1.5%，居住价格下降0.2%，生活用品及服务价格上涨1.4%，交通通信价格上涨2.0%，教
育文化娱乐价格上涨1.2%，医疗保健价格上涨2.3%，其他用品及服务价格上涨9.8%。在食品烟酒及在外餐饮价格中，猪肉价格下降12.9%，粮食价格下降0.4%，鲜果价格上涨0.7%，鲜菜价格上涨1.4%。9月份，全国居民消费价格同比上涨0.9%，环比上涨0.2%。前三季度，扣
除食品和能源价格后的核心CPI同比上涨1.1%。其中，9月份核心CPI同比上涨1.0%。前三季度，全国工业生产者出厂价格同比上涨2.3%。其中，9月份同比上涨5.0%，涨幅比上月扩大1.2个百分点；环比上涨1.2%。前三季度，全国工业生产者购进价格同比上涨3.6%。其中，9
月份同比上涨6.9%，环比上涨1.2%。
    就业：就业形势总体稳定，城镇调查失业率下降。前三季度，全国城镇调查失业率平均值为5.2%，与上年同期持平。9月份，全国城镇调查失业率为5.2%，比上月下降0.1个百分点。本地户籍劳动力调查失业率为5.2%；外来户籍劳动力调查失业率为5.1%，其中外来农业
户籍劳动力调查失业率为5.0%。31个大城市城镇调查失业率为5.2%，比上月下降0.1个百分点。全国企业就业人员周平均工作时间为48.3小时。三季度末，外出务工农村劳动力总量19270万人，同比增长0.4%。
    收入：居民收入平稳增长，农村居民收入增长快于城镇居民。前三季度，全国居民人均可支配收入34230元，同比名义增长5.3%，扣除价格因素实际增长4.4%。按常住地分，城镇居民人均可支配收入44945元，同比名义增长4.5%，实际增长3.6%；农村居民人均可支配收
入18809元，同比名义增长6.3%，实际增长5.6%。从收入来源看，全国居民人均工资性收入、经营净收入、财产净收入、转移净收入分别名义增长5.3%、6.6%、1.6%、5.7%。前三季度，城乡居民人均可支配收入比值（以农村居民为1）为2.39，同比缩小0.04。

总的来看，前三季度国民经济顶压前行、向新向优，主要指标积极变化增多，高质量发展取得新成效。也要看到，国际环境复杂多变，国内需求不足问题仍较突出，经济稳中向好基础尚需巩固。下阶段，要坚持以习近平新时代中国特色社会主义思想为指导，坚持
稳中求进工作总基调，完整准确全面贯彻新发展理念，加快构建新发展格局，坚持深化改革开放，强化创新驱动，着力发挥宏观政策效能，加力扩大内需、优化供给，切实保障和改善民生，推动经济向新向优向好发展。

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 10 月 14 日

1 群益投顧 
京元電 (2449 TT) 宣 布 與 NVIDIA  International, Inc. 簽訂預付款協議，支持美國全資
子公司 AeternZ 於 當 地 建 置 產 能 ， 但 現 階 段 仍 未 揭 露 合 作 金 額。 NVIDIA 於 07/2026
與 Amkor (AMKR US) 也 簽 約 相 似 的 預 付 款 合 約。顯 示 AI 晶 片 由 傳 統 單 一 系 統 單 晶 片
(SoC) 朝 Chiplet 及 異 質 整 合 發 展 ，封 測 複 雜 度 提 升 ，IC 設 計 廠 和 封 測 廠 合 作 深 化，
提 高 後 段 封 測 廠 訂 單 能 見 度 。 不 過 和NVIDIA 合作的此協議短期效益有限，京元電評
價合理 。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw  
京 元 電 宣 布 與 NVIDIA 簽 訂 預 付 款 協 議 ， 支 持 美 國 全 資 子 公 司 AeternZ 於
當 地 建 置 產 能，但 現 階 段 仍 未 揭 露 合 作 金 額：  
10/13/2026 京 元 電 重 訊 公 告 ， 董 事 會 決 議 通 過 與NVIDIA(NVDA US) 簽 訂 客
戶 預 付 款 協 議，將 由 NVIDIA 預 先 支 付 款 項，支 持 京 元 電 及 美 國 全 資 子 公 司
AeternZ Semiconductor Testing Corporation 在 美 國 建 置 測 試 產 能 ， 未 來 再 以
提 供 測 試 服 務 抵 減 預 付 款。京 元 電 同 步 宣 布，因 合 約 架 構 調 整，取 消 原 先 對
美 國 子 公 司 提 供 的 3 億 美 元 背 書 保 證 額 度 。 
京 元 電 此 次簽約 對 象 為 NVIDIA International, Inc. ， NVIDIA 預 付 款 項 ， 京
元 電 及 其 美 國 全 資 子 公 司 將 在 美 國 建 置 測 試 產 能 ， 為 NVIDIA 保 留 產 能 並
提 供 測 試 服 務，預 付 款 則 以 未 來 服 務 款 項 抵 減。此 合 作 並 非 股 權 投 資 或 合 資
模 式，而 是 在 既 有 客 戶 關 係 下，透 過 客 戶 提 前 提 供 資 金，支 持 供 應 商 擴 充 產
能 並 確 保 未 來 測 試 資 源 。 
京 元 電 董 事 會 已 於 07/ 2026 決 議 通 過 美 國 投 資 設 廠 案，規 劃 在 美 國 設 立 孫 公
司 並 興 建 新 廠 ， 投 資 金 額 以 14 億美元 (約 新 台 幣 450 億元 )為 上 限 。 公 司 當
時 表 示，此 投 資 主 要 因 應 未 來 營 運 需 求 及 全 球 供 應 鏈 布 局，後 續 將 依 設 廠 進
度 與 資 金 需 求 逐 步 投 入 ， 資 金 來 源 以 自 有 資 金 或 借 款 等 方 式 支 應 。 
此 次 協 議 有 助 於 提 高 新 增 產 能 的 需 求 能 見 度，並 降 低 部 分 初 期 資 金 壓 力。不
過，由 於 預 付 款 金 額、實 際 保 留 產 能 規 模 及 相 關 訂 單 金 額 尚 未 公 開，現 階 段
仍 無 法 量 化 對 營 收 與 獲 利 的 貢 獻；短 期 營 運 表 現 仍 取 決 於 既 有 產 能 擴 充、新
晶 片 測 試 需 求 及 美 國 廠 建 置 進 度 。 
NVIDIA 在 07/2026 已和 Amkor(AMKR US) 簽 訂 預 付 款 協 議 ， 約 15 億美
元 ：  
值 得 注 意 的 是 ，NVIDIA 於 07/ 2026 與 Amkor 簽訂約 15 億 美 元 的 多 年 期 先
進 封 裝 與 測 試 合 作 協 議，包 括 正 在 亞 利 桑 那 州 建 設 的 生 產 設 施。Amkor 目前
已為 NVIDIA 資 料 中 心 處 理 器 、 網 通 晶 片 組 及 加 速 運 算 系 統 提 供 先 進 封 裝
服務 。  
Amkor 於 2Q 26 法 說 會 表 示 ， 相 關 預 付 款 預 計 於 2027 年 收 取 ， 並 隨 後 續 提
供 服 務 逐 步 抵 減 ， 依 實 際 生 產 規 模 及 服 務 提 供 速 度 ， 預 估 合 作 期 間 約5~10
年 。 此 案 例 顯 示 ，NVIDIA 透 過 預 付 款 支 持 封 測 廠 擴 充 美 國 產 能 ， 並 非 短 期
採 購 安 排 ， 而 可 能 是 配 合 長 期 產 能 建 置 及 技 術 發 展 的 合 作 模 式 。 
京 元 電 此 次 亦 採 取和 Amkor 相 似 的 方 式 ，客 戶 預 付 款 、 建 置 美 國 產 能 並 以
未 來 測 試 服 務 款 項 抵 減。 Amkor 案 例 可 作 為 評 估 合 作 期 間 的 參 考。  
2 家 不 同 之 處，京 元 電 主 要 受 惠 於 高 階 測 試 需 求；Amkor 則 涉 及 先 進 封 裝 與

2 群益投顧 
測 試。兩 者 都 能 提 高 產 能 需 求 能 見 度，但 實 際 營 收 與 獲 利 貢 獻 仍 取 決 於 產 能
開 出 、 訂 單 規 模 及 服 務 條 件 。 
外 媒 報 導 京 元 電 美 國 子 公 司預 計 落 腳 德 州 沃 斯 堡 市(Fort Worth )：  
在 美 國 設 廠 地 點 方 面， 外 媒 於10/ 2026 報 導， 京 元 電 全 資 美 國 子 公 司AeternZ
正 評 估 於 德 州 沃 斯 堡(Fort Worth )設 立 半 導 體 測 試 廠，初 步 規 劃 投 資 約 13 億
美 元 ， 其 中 約 10 億 美 元 用 於 不 動 產 、3 億 美 元 用 於 設 備 ， 選 址 涉 及 West 
Everman Parkway 一 處 約 23 英 畝 的 土 地 。 沃 斯 堡 市 議 會 預 計 於 10 /27/2026
審 議 相 關 租 稅 優 惠，但 目 前 尚 不 能 視 為 京 元 電 已 正 式 決 定 設 廠 地 點，仍 須 觀
察 後 續 選 址 及 投 資 進 度。若 該 地 點 獲 選，德州相對 台 積 電 所 在 的 亞 利 桑 那 州
鳳 凰 城 聚 落 ，地 緣 上 相 對 鄰 近 AI 伺 服 器 供 應 鏈 ，代 表 AI 晶 片 完 成 測 試 後，
可 就 近 送 往 當 地 代 工 廠 ， 進 行 整 機 組 裝 與 系 統 測 試 。不 過 ， 廠 房 建 置 、 設 備
進 駐 及 客 戶 認 證 仍 需 時 間 ， 實 際 貢 獻 時 點 取 決 於 建 廠 進 度 與 測 試 需 求 。 
AI 晶 片 由 傳 統 單 一 系 統 單 晶 片(SoC) 朝 Chiplet 及 異 質 整 合 發 展， 封 測 複 雜
度 提 升 ， IC 設 計 廠 和 封 測 廠 合 作 深 化：  
隨著 AI 晶 片 由 傳 統 單 一 系 統 單 晶 片(SoC ) 朝 Chiplet 及 異 質 整 合 發 展 ， 半 導
體 產 業 的 競 爭 已 不 再 侷 限 於 晶 片 設 計 與 先 進 製 程，也 延 伸 至 先 進 封 裝、晶 粒
間 高 速 互 連、散 熱 及 測 試 驗 證 等 環 節。晶 片 架 構 複 雜 度 提 高，使 封 裝 與 測 試
成 為 影 響 產 品 效 能 、 成 本 及 量 產 能 力 的 重 要 因 素 。 
對 京 元 電 而 言，若 能 持 續 參 與 NVIDIA 新 晶 片 的 測 試 開 發 與 驗 證，並 承 接 後
續 量 產 測 試，有 助 於 深 化 客 戶 關 係 及 鞏 固 高 階 測 試 業 務 的 競 爭 優 勢；但 此 次
預付款協議本身並未改變雙方的股權關係，合作價值仍須透過後續訂單與
產 能 利 用 率 驗 證 。 
相 較 之 下 ， 力 成(6239 TT) 與 Broadcom (AVGO US) 規 劃 在 新 加 坡 成 立 先 進 封
裝 合 資 公 司 ， 發 展 扇 出 型 面 板 級 封 裝 （FOPLP ）及 重 布 線 層 （RDL ） 等 相 關
技術，屬於 IC 設 計 公 司 直 接 參 與 封 裝 製 造 平 台 投 資 的 模 式 。 此 合 作 不 僅 涉
及 產 能 配 置，也 涵 蓋 新 技 術 開 發 與 量 產 導 入，反 映 當 先 進 封 裝 成 為 影 響 晶 片
效 能 、 互 連 密 度 及 量 產 成 本 的 關 鍵 環 節 ，IC 設 計 公 司 可 能 更 積 極 參 與 封 裝
技 術 與 產 能 布 局。對 力 成 而 言，若 能 順 利 完 成 製 程 驗 證、良 率 提 升 及 量 產 導
入，有 機 會 拓 展 新 的 技 術 平 台 與 業 務 成 長 機 會；惟 仍 須 觀 察 客 戶 訂 單、量 產
進 度 、 資 本 支 出 及 投 資 報 酬 率 。 
整 體 而 言 ，NVIDIA 與 京 元 電 、Amkor 的 合 作 ， 主 要 是 透 過 客 戶 預 付 款 支 持
產 能 建 置 ， 強 化 供 應 商 的 需 求 與 資 金 能 見 度 ；Broadcom 與 力 成 的 合 作 ， 則
是 透 過 共 同 投 資 發 展 新 的 先 進 封 裝 技 術 平 台 。 兩 者 反 映AI 半 導 體 產 業 的 合
作 模 式 正 逐 步 深 化，但 資 金 安 排、技 術 參 與 程 度 及 投 資 風 險 並 不 相 同。投 資
上，京 元 電 應 關 注 預 付 款 規 模、美 國 新 廠 投 產 及 高 階 測 試 訂 單 成 長；力 成 則
應 觀 察 先 進 封 裝 技 術 商 業 化、量 產 良 率 與 合 資 事 業 的 投 資 報 酬。京 元 電 此 次
協 議 對 中 長 期 業 務 發 展 具 正 面 意 義，但 在 實 際 財 務 貢 獻 尚 未 明 朗 前，宜 視 為
客 戶 關 係 深 化 及 產 能 布 局 的 正 面 訊 號，而 非 直 接 上 修 獲 利 預 估 的 依 據。由於
目 前 京 元 電 評 價 合 理 ，投 資 建 議 Neutral 。

產業速報

4 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 3

TSMC (2330.TW)
15 October 2026 Citi Research
3
Rather than now slowing down, it’s actually getting stronger
TSMC reported an upbeat 3Q26 result with robust 4Q26 outlook. It guided for
4Q26 revenue of US$52.3–54.3 billion, representing about 14% QoQ growth and
58% year-over-year growth at the midpoint (based on FX at NT$31.8 per USD).
4Q26 GM/OPM is expected at 65–67% and 56.5–58.5%. TSMC reiterated its 2026
capex to be US$60–64 billion and said investment from 2026 to 2028 will be

significantly higher than in the previous three years, reflecting strong multi-year
customer demand, including agentic AI. It is also worth noting that TSMC plans to
begin using the High-NA EUV technology around 2030 and develop a 12-inch
mask capability by 2031, when it expects the tools to offer sufficient maturity and
cost effectiveness. Management said this timing does not undermine its
confidence in technology leadership.
Management described AI-related demand as exceptionally strong, supported by

accelerators, networking chips, and rising demand for CPUs associated with
agentic AI. TSMC’s previously guided AI GPU/accelerator CAGR to be mid-to-high
50%, but now sees more upside ahead. Given rising concern on AI expanding
peaking out, the management said it is clear that its capacity planning strategy has
considered customer forecasts, data-center construction, power availability, and
other supply-chain constraints. Management still judges demand to be above

available supply and is working to close the gap, while emphasizing disciplined
planning to limit the risk of overbuilding.
Figure 1. TSMC – 3Q26 results comparison
© 2026 Citigroup Inc. No redistribution without Citigroup’s written permission.
Source: Citi Research, Bloomberg
Capacity expansion plan and allocation
During the earnings call, TSMC also mentioned that despite the tight capacity
supply, customers cannot generally sublet or auction wafer capacity, while there is

limited case that collaboration is through mask-license agreements. For the
advanced packaging, TSMC has announced its Global Foundries and VIS’
agreement on interposer as part of its backend supply strategy, intended to help
ensure customers have enough capacity to bring products to market; It also
reiterated that TSMC’s CoWoS remains as the leading customer’s choice and is
already in volume production at 5x reticle size, while it is developing 9x and larger

solutions and evaluating CoPoS.

### Page 4

TSMC (2330.TW)
15 October 2026 Citi Research
4
Reiterate Buy, best position in AI arena
Our earnings projection is largely unchanged for 2027 /2028 earnings. While its
capex expansion, overseas operation and N2 will likely be a margin dilution factor
initially, due to its cross-node leverage, high UTR and yield rate, we believe its GM
downside is manageable. Overall, we see strong advanced node demand into next
year and the long-term order visibility will be supported for higher capex cycle.  We

maintain our TP to NT$4,000, based on unchanged 25x of 2027 EPS estimate;
reiterate Buy.
Key TSMC earnings call recap
n4Q26 guidance
–Sales: US$52.3-54.3bn (+14% QoQ at the midpoint)
–GPM: 65-67% (assuming USDTWD=31.8)
–OPM: 56.5-58.5%
n2026 full-year & long-term guidance
–Full year sales: raise 2026 full-year USD-based sales guidance from more
than 40% growth to +44% growth
–Full year capex: retain its 2026 capex guidance at US$60-64bn (70-80%
advanced process, 10% specialty, 10-20% for advanced packaging, testing

and masks)
–GM outlook
•4Q GM to be impacted by N2 and overseas fab ramps, but partially
supported by productivity improvement, UTR enhancement and
optimizing capacity allocation
•GM dilution from overseas fab would be c2ppts impact for the full-year
2026; with more new capacity expansion in the US, the margin dilution
impact would widen to 3-4ppts in the next few years.
–Long-term outlook
•Retains 5-year AI sales CAGR (2024-2029) remains at high-50% or above

nCapacity planning
–TSMC currently has 20 fabs in total. It plans to add 13 fabs in Taiwan and add
5-6 overseas fabs.
–N2 capacity will double in 2027; it has also started preparing for A14 capacity
with volume production starting from 2028.
–US Arizona fab 2 construction is complete and begin volume production in
3Q27, ahead of schedule.
–JP fab 2 would produce N3 with mass production from 2028

### Page 10

TSMC (2330.TW)
15 October 2026 Citi Research
10
TSMC
Company description
TSMC is the founder and leader of the dedicated IC foundry segment. The
company has built its reputation by offering advanced and "more-than-
Moore" wafer production processes and industry-leading manufacturing
efficiency. From its inception in 1987, TSMC has consistently offered the
foundry segment's leading technologies and TSMC-compatible design
services.
Investment strategy

We rate TSMC shares as Buy. We expect TSMC to deliver sales growth of
>40% in recent years thanks to strong growth from HPC/AI as well as stable
demand growth from smartphone, Io T, and automotive on tech migration and
a shortened replacment cycle. CPU outsourcing to TSMC offers further
growth potential. A rising cash dividend should also support the share price.
While TSMC has been increasing its capex meaningfully since 2009, its cost
structure remaisn competitive through continuous product mix

improvements, efficiency enhancements, and cost reductions.
Valuation
Our target price for TSMC shares of NT$4,000 is based on 25x our 2027E
EPS, higher than its three-year average forward PER, versus global peers’
average of 16-25x. We believe our target PER is justified by TSMC's leading
position in advanced process node and robust AI demand growth outlook.
Given its continuing leadership in the semiconductor foundry industry, we
believe TSMC’s stable order visibility and earnings outlook make it less

vulnerable to a potential global economic downturn than some of its peers.
Our target price equates to 2026E/27E P/E of ~ 36x/25x and 2026E/27E P/B of
~13x/10x.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from achieving our target
price include: 1) weakness in the global semiconductor market; 2) larger-
than-expected margin contractions due to depreciation cost increases and
strong NTD; 3) competitors entering the foundry business; 4) longer-than-

expected digestion of inventory in the supply chain; and 5) a slowdown in
demand either due to a global economic downturn or a global trade
disruption triggered by tariffs.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: TSMC continued delivering “beat-n-raise” prints for its 
quarterly results and guidance on 15 October. While we raise our forecasts 
to factor in its better topline and pricing trajectory yet muted margin 
expectation on an incremental basis, we see likely lack of near-term 
passion from investors on limited positive surprises from its results call this 
time. That said, we believe AI demand remains strong and sustainable to 
support our Buy (1) call on the stock, though patience may be necessary 
for investors amid the current jittery environment.

What’s the impact: Results intact, guidance mixed. Despite 3Q26 
topline of USD46.7bn topping guidance (USD44.6-45.8bn) at TSMC, its 
3Q26 EPS came in at TWD29.4, only marginally beating our/Bloomberg 
consensus estimates on lower opex and higher non-operating gains from 
long-term investments more than offsetting lower gross margin associated 
with higher depreciation costs on dilutive N2 ramp (table on p.2). Guidance 
calls for higher topline for 4Q26 with midpoint of the range 6% above our 
previous forecast, thanks to continued AI strength as customers continue to 
increase demand. Yet margins at the operating level looked muted without 
further operating leverage being expected by the street, due to the margin-
dilutive N2 ramp. We raise our 2026-28E forecasts by 3-10% to factor in 
the better topline trajectory, plus our view of better pricing power.

Lack of near-term passion? Apart from the upbeat topline but muted 
margin guidance on an incremental basis, we left the results call with 
limited positive surprises this time which may not drive any passion for 
investors in the near term to catalyse the stock. That said, fundamentals 
remain strong since AI demand continues to mushroom from accelerators 
to CPU and other companion chips, as language models transform from 
generative to agentic for AI assistance. TSMC implied upside risk to its 
long-term AI accelerator revenue CAGR of mid-to-high-50% over 2024-29 
and, as flagged in our Asian Foundry report, we saw upside risk to TSMC’s 
pricing power for 2027, given the still meaningful supply constraint where 
management reiterated its strong conviction in this multi-year AI megatrend 
and active capacity build aimed to narrow the supply/demand gap.

What we recommend: We raise our 12M TP for TSMC to TWD3,200 
(previous: TWD3,000), based on our finetuned ROE-adjusted PBR target of 
7.5x one-year forward BVPS forecast (previous: 7.7x). We reiterate our Buy 
(1) call with key downside risks of “silicon inflation” and geopolitical 
overhangs leading to global inflation and AI capex cut.

How we differ: Our 2028E EPS is 11% below the Bloomberg consensus 
likely on our view calling for a soft-landing in the chip cycle.

15 October 2026

Lack of passion despite strong AI?

➢ Another “beat-n-raise” in quarterly prints but likely … 
➢ … short of catalysts on limited positive surprise this time 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call with a higher 12M TP of TWD3,200

Company profile 
Incorporated in Taiwan in 1987, Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co. (TSMC) is the world’s largest 
complementary metal oxide semiconductor (CMOS) foundry in revenue terms. TSMC offers foundry 
services such as wafer masking, fabrication, probing, packaging and testing, to a high variety of 
customers including fabless chipmakers and IDMs. Its manufacturing fabs are located in Taiwan, China, 
the US and Singapore.

### Page 5

5

Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing (2330 TT): 15 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 15 Oct 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
TSMC's board of directors (BoD) has 10 members, including 6 independent directors
representing 60% of total directors. The background of TSMC's BoD is diversified, with members
from industries, academia, and law. Also, its board members include representatives from not
only Taiwan but also Europe and the US, bringing their experience in operating international
businesses to the board.
We believe the high percentage of independent directors and a diverse mix of expertise from the
board members strengthen the functionality of its BoD, as evidenced by TSMC's leading position
in the foundry market.
Capital management 1
TSMC pays its dividend on a quarterly basis. In the past 5 years, it has managed its dividend
payout well, as evidenced by its dividend in dollar terms, where it has never lowered its dividend.
As the leader in advanced technology nodes, TSMC has kept expanding its capacity for
advanced nodes; as such, we expect its capital intensity to increase to the level near or above
40% over 2025-26 (vs. its long-term target of mid-30%). However, we believe TSMC will at least
sustain its cash dividend payout in dollar terms over 2025-26E. We see its balance between
investment and dividend as appropriate.
Related party &
transaction 1
TSMC's sales to related parties and purchases from related parties remain immaterial as a
percentage of total revenue and cost of goods sold in 2026. We view these levels as insignificant
and therefore see limited risk to TSMC's earnings, cash flow generation, or corporate
governance.
S Supply chainmanagement 1
As the leader in the global foundry industry, TSMC approaches supply chain management by
focusing on 2 main strategies: sustainability risk management and local supply chain
optimisation. It requires all its suppliers to comply with its "Code with Ethics and Business
Conduct", and to follow regulations on human rights and conflict-free minerals.
In 2019, 100% of its tier-1 suppliers signed the "Supplier Code of Conduct" and self-assessment
questionnaire on sustainable management. Also, TSMC's raw materials purchased are 100%
Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) conflict-free. It also requires its critical suppliers to
report on the status of sustainability management in their critical upstream supply chains.
Through these measures, we see TSMC reducing its supply chain risk.
E Water & wastewatermanagement 2
TSMC applies internal and external company resources to develop water reclaimation
technology, implements water conservation measures and uses reeclaimed water in the
manufacturing process. For example, on September 19, 2022, TSMC Tainan Science Park
Reclaimed Water Plant started supplying reclaimed water. In 2025, TSMC aims to replace 5% of
water resources with reclaimed water, and continue to cooperate with the government to
complete the 2nd water reclamation plan in Anping, Tainan. In 2030, TSMC targets to replace >
60% of water resources with reclaimed water.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
TSMC's waste recycling rate reached 96% in 2022 for 8 years in a row. Also, the percentage of
waste sent to landfills was less than 1% for 13 years in a row in 2022.
TSMC achieved reducing its outsourced unit waste disposal (kg/12" equivalent wafer mask
layer) to 0.99 in 2022. Furthermore, TSMC has set a goal to reduce its outsourced unit waste
disposal per wafer to no more than 0.5 in 2030.
E GHG emissions 1
TSMC has continued its efforts to reduce greenhouse gas (GHG) emissions to drive low-carbon
manufacturing. In 2022, TSMC achieved net zero emissions of direct and indirect (from
purchased energy) GHG Emissions in overseas factories, and 100% use of
carbon-neutral natural gas in Taiwan fabs. It has set a target to achieve Net Zero Emissions by
2050.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

MediaTek

ASIC business expanding into Bytedance; 
potential high-end mobile gains in ‘27

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 6,200 TWD | Price: 4,475 TWD

Bytedance expanding ASIC TAM beyond Google/xAI 
Bloomberg reported on October 15th that Bytedance is close to an agreement to work 
with Mediatek on the ASIC design and turnkey service support following its collaboration 
with Broadcom and Qualcomm. This is consistent with our update in September that 
Mediatek could co-develop an AI inference chipset with Bytedance from 1H27 for tape 
out in 1H28 and see contribution from production in early ’29. Similar to Mediatek and 
xAI’s collaboration, we believe Bytedance would need the support on sourcing 
components (e.g. HBM and substrate) in addition to wafer manufacturing/packaging, 
lifting the dollar content vs. Google’s TPUs. Assuming 1mn unit demand annually & 
US$20-25k ASPs including advanced logic dies and HBM, the project could contribute 
US$20-25bn to Mediatek’s sales in ’29 (vs. BofA sales expectation on Mediatek  of 
US$82bn in ’28 with US$45bn from Google TPUv8/v9 and US$9bn from xAI’s 1-2 
quarter contribution). 
A modest tailwind in mobile as it gains share at Samsung 
The mobile chipset builds from Mediatek and Qualcomm in 2H26 are tracking below 
seasonal in 2H26 as the end demand is impacted by the elevated memory cost. While 
the market outlook looms into ’27 (vs. -25% Android unit demand in ’26), we expect 
Mediatek’s mobile business to fare better especially in the high-end segment where it 
could gain unit share by penetrating into Samsung’s flagship smartphones historically 
dominated by Qualcomm and Samsung’s own SoCs. Factoring 30% share in Samsung’s 
high-end mobile shipment of 30-35mn annual units and US$200+ chipset ASPs, it could 
be ~20% accretive to Mediatek’s mobile sales and ~5% of its total business from 2H27. 
EPS and valuation downside limited after recent pullback 
We maintain our Buy rating & NT$6,200 PO on 24x 2H27-1H28E P/E (implied 30x ASIC 
EPS + 10x merchant EPS). The share has been dragged by TPU competition concern in 
the near term but could be lifted by expanding ASIC projects and high-end mobile gains.

Key Metrics      
* For full definitions of iQmethod SM measures, see page 6.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
MediaTek is a fabless IC design company supplying to 
wireless communications and digital consumer markets. 
Before taking leadership in mobile SoC area, it had built 
solid ground in the TV SoC and optical disk drive markets. 
The firm invests heavily in R&D to seek better positioning 
in the 5G cycle, and has a complete offering including ASIC, 
Wi-Fi, PMIC etc. to address applications beyond 
smartphone, such as IoT and auto.

Investment Rationale 
We have Buy rating on MediaTek as we think MediaTek is 
ready and has the strength to brace for the upcoming 
proliferation of IoT devices and opportunities in growing 
markets like ASIC, ARM-based PC, auto, and etc., and we 
believe its solid balance sheet and cashflow could provide 
downside support to the share price. Meanwhile, we expect 
5G smartphone penetration to be a multi-year theme for 
the company, with its better competitive landscape in 
duopoly 5G era and an improved margin profile.

### Page 3

Focus charts and tables

Price objective basis & risk 
MediaTek (MDTKF) 
Our PO of NT$6,200 is based on 24x 2H27-1H28 P/E. which is towards the higher end 
of its historical P/E range of 8-25x, as we believe the company's broadening ASIC 
pipeline could drive an earnings upgrade cycle.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) better than expected smartphone demand, especially in 
China, (2) a better pricing in mobile communication segment thanks to alleviated 
competition and rising exposure to high-end/flagship market, and (3) an even more 
superior investment return on non-op side to uplift its net cash position. (4) Unexpected 
collaboration with global technology leaders leads to higher than expected 
margins/profits

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) weaker-than-expected end-market situation in 
consumer-related smartphone/TV space, (2) macro challenges including inflation/policy-
related pressures, (3) rising competition from existing players and/or newcomers in 
smartphone/TV SoC (system on chip) space, and (4) ASP/margin pressure caused by each 
or all of the above and/or rising foundry costs. (4) Bidding process for Meta Inference 
ASIC is slower than expected.

of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment rating s are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation).

not stretched considering the sustainable growth of WNC driven by long-term satellite 
and high speed networking demand. Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology: WNC 
Valuation: We use a near-term P/E to derive our 12m target price for WNC, consistent 
with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our TP of NT$380.0 on a target P/E 
multiple of 28.0x on a forward year EPS (2027E). Our target P/E is derived from the 
correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers. We are Buy rated on WNC. Key 
downside risks: fiercer-than-expected competition in satellite communication, 
slower-than-expected Wi-Fi 7 or 5G FWA ramp-up in US and Europe markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that

樺漢 (6414 TT) 
在手訂單維持 2550 億元

永豐觀點 
三大事業體持續成長，第三季營收同期新高，未來營運將聚焦於 Physical AI 市場
發展。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進投資建議：主係考量(1) 實體 AI 應用落地，包含 AMR、自動駕駛、智慧
工廠、機器人等應用，樺漢從產品、平台、軟硬整合延伸，潛在市場成長空間相當
大；(2) Kontron 完成整併，策略整合綜效逐漸顯現，擴大競爭優勢；(3) 除三大
事業體接單動能穩健外， 智能零售市場成長性高，樺漢將從硬體製造跨足到軟體平
台 ， 目前合作對象皆外國內外知名餐飲及零售商等 。 評價方面 ， 以2027 年稅後 EPS 
28.5 元，目前本益比約 14 倍，過往歷史本益比區間為 13~22 倍，考量在手訂單
持續墊高，營運規模效益可期，故維持買進的投資建議，目標價485 元(17X 2027 
EPS)。

ESG 評析 
樺漢企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B 等與 A 等。

2

樺漢為全球工業電腦大廠：樺漢科技股份有限公司成立於 1999 年，為鴻海轉投資
工業電腦廠，產品包括 POS(point of sale)系統、ATM 系統、工業控制系統、車載
系統 、 人機互動式產品和其他周邊如處理器 、 記憶體等產品 。 最大股東為鴻海集團 ，
持有 24.1%股權(包括寶鑫國際 22.7%、鴻揚創投 1.4%)。樺漢主要分成三大事業
群：工業物聯網、智慧軟體與方案、智慧工廠與廠務。2026 年 1-9 月營收占比：
工業物聯網 45.7%、智慧工廠與廠務 42.6％、智慧軟體與方案 11.7%。 
邊緣運算及半導體應用， 3Q26 營收單季新高 ：樺漢 3Q26 營收 496.4 億元
(+2.6%QoQ，+47.4%YoY)， 成長主係來自於工業物聯網中邊緣運算需求強勁以及
智慧工廠受惠於半導體客戶廠務工程進度持續推薦 ， 智慧軟體與方案部門因整併及
專 案 進 度 影 響 略 微 衰 退， 研 究 部 預 估 在 產 品 組 合 差 異 不 大 下 ， 毛 利 率
19.5%(+0.2pts QoQ，-2.2ptsYoY)，營利率 5.9%(+0.3ptsQoQ，+2.1ptsYoY)，
稅後淨利 10.35 億元(-1.5%QoQ，+8.9%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 6.59 元。 
訂單能見度佳，2026 年高成長不變：展望第四季，樺漢維持全年高成長目標，三
大事業體出貨狀況良好，其中 NCRV 全年預估貢獻 160 億元，Kontron 亦完成收
購並持續深化營運綜效，智慧工廠業務訂單將逐季認列營收。研究部預估樺漢
4Q26 營收 519.9 億元(+4.7%QoQ，+31.6%YoY)，毛利率 19.6%(+0.1ptsQoQ，
-1ptsYoY)，營利率 6.7%(+0.8ptsQoQ，-1.2ptsYoY)，稅後淨利 11.37 億元
(+9.8%QoQ，+30.6%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 7.24 元。預估 2026 年營收 1871.3 億
元 (+31.5%YoY)， 受 產 品 組 合 影 響 ， 毛 利 率19.6%(-0.9pts YoY) ， 營 利 率
6%(+1.4pts YoY)，稅後淨利 39.75 億元(+23.7%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 25.32 元。 
在手訂單 2550 億元，朝向成為高毛利軟硬整合系統商：樺漢集團策略朝向聚焦高
毛利高成長事業，如從 Physical AI 相關產業進行全面整合，以成為系統整合商為
營運重點，目前在手訂單維持 2550 億高檔並持續推升。此外，持續整合Kontron
營運創造縱效，透過技術整合及市場聚焦，管理層目標 Kontron2030 年為營收達
26 億歐元、EBITDA 目標 4.2 億歐元，並透過持續降低供應鏈採購成本及製造成
本，擴大營運槓桿優勢，提高軟體等高價值產品。獲利方面，預估高毛利產品營收
占比將持續提升，產業聚焦於零售餐飲、智慧能源與樓宇、交通、醫療長照、智慧
城市與公共安全、智慧製造等。展望未來，預估 2027 年 NCRV 營收年增 15%，
智慧零售將成為下一個成長動能 ，樺漢將打造平台的生台戲 ， 從方案代理商製產品
銷售並與 Nvidia 及聯發科技術合作。長期來看，無論是透過策略合作或者併購，

樺漢在供應鏈、生產和管理成本上都將達到合作的綜效；智慧工廠與廠務(帆宣)方
面，北美半導體擴大投資，將受惠於主要客戶前進全球設廠及既有產能擴張，需求
尚屬穩健。近期樺漢集團透過組織重整，將集團整合並增強綜效，增加平台及軟硬
體營收，優化獲利能力。另一方面，實體 AI 市場持續擴大中，樺漢已有許多專案
開發的實績，預估將受惠此趨勢的成長，研究員預估 2027 年營收 2074.8 億元
(+10.9%YoY)，毛利率 20%(+0.4pts YoY)，營利率 6.7%(+0.7pts YoY)，稅後淨
利 44.75 億元(+12.6%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 28.5 元。

9月经济数据快报
前三季度国内生产总值1071071亿元，按不变价格计算，同比增长4.6%。分产业看，第一产业
增加值58994亿元，同比增长3.7%；第二产业增加值383822亿元，增长3.8%；第三产业增加值
628255亿元，增长5.2%。分季度看，一季度国内生产总值同比增长5.0%，二季度增长4.3%，三
季度增长4.5%。从环比看，三季度国内生产总值增长1.2%。

前三季度，全国规模以上工业增加值同比增长5.3%。分三大门类看，采矿业增加值同比增长
2.0%，制造业增长5.7%，电力、热力、燃气及水生产和供应业增长5.4%。装备制造业增加值同比
增长10.2%，高技术制造业增加值增长14.3%，分别快于全部规模以上工业增加值4.9和9.0个百分
点。
前三季度，全国固定资产投资（不含农户）334115亿元，同比下降7.2%，与1—8月份持平；扣
除房地产开发的固定资产投资下降4.2%。其中，知识产权产品投资同比增长9.3%。高技术产业投
资同比增长4.9%。分产业看，第一产业投资同比下降3.9%，第二产业投资下降2.7%，第三产业投
资下降10.0%。
前三季度，货物进出口总额399399亿元，同比增长18.8%。其中，出口231979亿元，增长
16.4%；进口167420亿元，增长22.3%。对共建“一带一路”国家进出口增长17.1%。民营企业进
出口增长19.1%，占进出口总额的比重为56.9%。机电产品出口增长23.7%，占出口总额的比重为

宏观金融团队 范丽军 Z0018336

3Q26Pre: 환율 하락에도 성장 흐름 유지 
매출액 1조 3,477억원(+7.2%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 5,957억원(-6.0%, OPM 
44.2%)로 컨센서스에 부합할 것으로 전망. 3분기 원달러 평균 환율은 1,423원으로 전분기 
평균 환율 대비 약 5.2% 하락에도 불구하고 성장 흐름 지속. 
2분기 노조 파업으로 인한 영향은 3분기 생산을 진행했으며 4분기 매출에 반영될 예정으로 
연간 실적에 미치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상. GSK로부터 인수한 록빌 공장도 안정적
으로 생산이 이루어지고 있으며 CAPA 여유분은 추가 수주를 진행 중으로 28~29년 이후 
완전 가동 전망. 
4Q, 6공장 건설과 폴리펩타이드 인수 
유상 증자 관련 투자 설명서 상 투자 계획에 따르면 6공장 투자는 26년부터 29년까지 진행 
될 예정으로 29년 가동 목표. 6공장은 5공장과 동일한 18만 리터 규모로 건설되며, 연내 투
자 집행을 시작함에 따라 건설 발표도 임박한 것으로 추정. 동사는 일정 수준 이상 기존 공
장 수주를 확보한 후 후속 공장 건설을 진행해오고 있어 non-binding 수주 계약의 본계약 
전환도 순차적으로 이루어질 것으로 전망. 
폴리펩타이드 인수도 현재 공개 매수를 진행 중에 있으며 연내 마무리될 것으로 예상. 
1H26 폴리펩타이드 매출은 2.37억 유로(+41.6%)로 GLP-1 agonist 등 펩타이드 의약품 
수요 증가에 따라 높은 성장 지속. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원으로 하향 
매수 의견을 유지하며 목표 주가는 기존 210만원에서 200만원으로 하향. 목표 주가 하향은 
유상 증자에 따른 EPS 희석에 기인. 글로벌 항체 CDMO 수요가 여전히 견조하며, 폴리펩
타이드 인수로 펩타이드로의 확장에 따른 추가적인 성장 기대. 주주 대상 유상 증자 영향과 
파업 우려 등이 있으나 노사 합의 시 주가 반등 전망.

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 드디어 엔진 증설, 저평가된 주가HD현대중공업(329180) 드디어 엔진 증설, 저평가된 주가드디어 엔진 증설, 저평_dc1151a4.pdf

### Page 1

2026.09.11  
HD 현대중공업 (329180)

드디어 엔진 증설, 저평가된 주가  Flash Note 
[조선/기계/방산] 변용진 2122-9181 yongjin.byun@imfnsec.com

Check Point  Buy (Maintain) 
HD현대중공업 중형엔진 및 SMR 공장 증설 결정  
엔진도 끌어올리지 못하는 조선업 주가, 그래도 펀더멘털은 굳건

목표주가(12M) 860,000 원(유지) 
종가(2026.09.10) 453,500 원 
상승여력 89.6 %

HD현대중공업, 중형엔진 및 SMR 시설투자 공시  
기다리던 엔진 증설의 세부 사항이 드디어 공개되었다. HD 현대중공업은 10일 공시를 통해 총 1조 722억원(중형엔진 
8,336억원/SMR 2,386억원) 규모의 시설투자 계획을 밝혔다. 먼저 중형엔진 관련, 글로벌 전력 수요 증가에 따른 엔진발전 시장 
확대에 대응 목적으로 생산능력 확충을 위한 토지 취득 및 공장 신설 및 생산설비 구축에 28년 5월까지 투자가 진행될 예정이다. 
중형엔진의 생산 능력은 현재 연간 3GW급에서 증설이 완료되면 7.2GW급으로 확장되며, 첫 엔진 출하는 28년 하반기로 예상된다. 
증설되는 신공장의 가동률은 28년 30%로 출발하여 30년 100%로 올라설 전망이다. 이에 따라 연간 1GW당 약 1조원의 매출과 
20%이상의 영업이익(현 엔진사업부 이상) 수준으로 추정되며, 계획대로 증설이 완료되고 일감을 확보한다면 총 4조원 이상의 매출 
증대 효과가 기대된다. SMR 투자 관련해서는 SMR 주기기 제작사업 진입 및 수주물량 증가에 대응하기 위해 SMR 전용 공장, 설비 
구축 및 기존 공장 최적화에 29년 4월까지 2,386억원이 투입된다. 이는 연간으로 Terrapower의 Natrium SMR 주기기 2기(각 
345MW)를 제작할 수 있는 규모다. HD현대중공업은 이번 증설이 데이터센터용 엔진만을 위한 것이 아님을 강조했다. 데이터센터 뿐 
아니라 국가전력망, 원전용 비상발전기 등 전세계적으로 증가하는 육상용 발전엔진 수요에 대응하기 위함이며, 최근 미국의 육상 
데이터센터 사업이 님비현상 등으로 일부 좌초되고 있음에도 그보다 더 큰 그림에 베팅한 것이다.  
엔진도 끌어올리지 못하는 조선업 주가, 그래도 펀더멘털은 굳건  
분명한 기업가치 증분의 근거가 되는 증설공시였음에도 불구하고 HD현대중공업의 주가는 발표 이후 하락으로 마감하였다. 올해 
조선업 주가는 YTD 평균 10%이상 하락하며 약세를 보이고 있는 상태다. (HD현대중공업 -10.0%, 삼성중공업 -11.6%, 한화오션 -
25.5%)  
조선사 공히 2분기의 실적 서프라이즈와 함께 올해 수주목표를 사실상 초과 달성하는 등 우수한 펀더멘털을 유지하고 있으나 이는 
주가 상승재료로 반영되고 있지 않다. 7~8월 반도체발 주식시장 혼란에 따른 전체적 수급 약세도 무시할 수 없는 요인이다. 여기에 
지난해 주가 상승의 주요 동력으로 작용하였던 미국 함정 사업 관련 협력 진전이 느린 점 등이 주가 약세의 주요 원인으로 
분석된다. 백악관 및 美해군에서는 지속적으로 함정 해외 건조의 필요성을 역설하고 있으나 美입법부에서는 지역구 의원의 반대 
등으로 관련 법안의 입법 동력이 약해져 있는 상황이다. 美국회의 이번 회기(119대 회기, ~2027년 1월 3일까지)가 이미 막바지에 
다다르고 있으며 11월 중간선거 일정까지 감안하면 실질적으로 이번 회기중에 관련 법안이 통과 및 발효될 가능성은 미미하기 
때문에, 2026년 남은 하반기에 함정사업 관련 조선업 주가 동력을 기대하기는 힘들어졌다. 다가오고 있는 3분기 실적도 원화 강세 
및 여름휴가/사고 등에 따른 조업일수 하락, 후판 유통가 상승 등 감익 요인이 산적해 있어 2분기에 비해 쉽지 않은 상황이다.  
그러나 여전히 본업인 조선업의 체력은 훌륭하다. 전세계 선박 발주량은 8월 누적기준 5,972만 CGT로 전년동기 대비 +60.6% 많은 
발주가 나오고 있으며 이는 2021년 이후 가장 강한 발주세다. 한국 조선사의 주력 선종인 LNG, 탱커, 컨테이너선의 발주 비중이 
전체 발주량의 67%를 차지하고 있으며 이에 힘입어 조선사들은 수주목표를 사실상 초과 달성하였다. (상선기준, HD현대중공업 달성 
130.6억 달러/목표 114.7억, 삼성중공업 달성 61억/목표 57억, 한화오션 달성 60억/목표없음) 3분기 실적은 다소 쉬어갈 수 
있으나 여전히 수주잔고에 기반한 추정상 매출/영업이익은 2028년까지 꾸준한 상승이 예상된다. 작년보다 오히려 하락한 주가가 
현저한 저평가일 수밖에 없는 이유다. 조선업 비중확대 및 HD현대중공업 Top Pick 의견을 유지한다.

 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

### Page 3

28 September 2026
3
Summary
Executive
• Summer brought little of the calm markets had priced in before it began. Three
tensions we had been tracking escalated through the season, surfacing as fresh
flashpoints by early September: a fresh energy shock out of the Middle East, a bond
sell-off that has spread from the US to Japan to Europe (and drawn erratic policy
responses from central banks and governments alike) and renewed doubts about the AI
boom – talk of chip and power shortages, bubble concerns and underwhelming returns

on frontier-model spend.
• But none of this has yet broken the real economy. Q2 growth surprised to the
upside, carried by net exports and Germany‘s stimulus in Europe, and by robust AI-
related investment in the US and Asia. Forward-looking indicators such as European
manufacturing PMIs at four-year highs and strong US Q3 nowcasts show little sign of
deterioration, albeit with marked divergence across sectors.
• The Great Unwind: the case for an orderly path forward. Our baseline continues to

be that these three tension points will ease rather than escalate, but that call rests
on specific mechanisms. In the report we also model three downside scenarios and
their economic and capital market impacts, each based on a disorderly release of the
tensions.
o The energy shock risk. We expect the conflict to stay contained and that
rerouting, demand destruction and alternative supply do the job energy markets
need them to do, easing prices and letting the inflation shock fade. This rests on

the assumption that the Saudi pipeline outage does not extend past a month. If
the outage runs longer, the shortfall could exceed 8Mb/d and Brent could stay
near USD100/bbl, turning what is now a contained shock into a stagflationary
shock.
o The bond sell-off risk. We assume inflation moderates without meaningful
second-round wage effects, and that sovereign markets can absorb elevated
issuance without a disorderly rise in risk premia.  The assumption doing the real

work here is political: the US midterms don‘t produce further fiscal slippage or
erratic policy interventions and European elections (France, Italy) do not add fiscal
uncertainty. Note that the real rate component of the US 10 treasury rates has
increased by a full percentage point this past year confirming that bond markets
also price more AI growth. In the downside, self-reinforcing yield–fiscal loop can
take hold and force central banks to stabilize bond markets.

o The AI capex cycle risk. We think that AI capex decelerates slightly to peak at
USD1trn in 2028, with value creation shifting from infrastructure build-out toward
adoption and productivity. This rests on the assumption that infrastructure and
power bottlenecks stay manageable, financing conditions don‘t tighten sharply
and AI earnings keep validating the spend. A sharper investment retrenchment,
given how much of US and Asian growth and equity performance now runs

### Page 4

Allianz Research
4
• Our scenario in numbers: Global GDP growth slows from +3.0% in 2025 to +2.6%
in 2026, recovers to +2.8% in 2027, and eases again to +2.6% in 2028. US growth
moderates from +2.1% (2026) to +1.8% (2028); the Eurozone recovers from +1.0% to
+1.3%; Asia stays the main engine at around +4.4%. We forecast Brent to average 87
USD/bbl in Q4 2026 before easing to 76 USD/bbl on average in 2027 as Gulf export
flows gradually normalize both in and around Hormuz but refined-product markets

are running out of buffers, and prices should remain elevated for the next few quarters.
European gas prices should stay elevated through year-end, with TTF averaging around
EUR65/MWh in Q4 2026, before easing to about EUR36/MWh in 2027. Inflation stays
1–2pps above target in Europe and the US until H2 2027, which is why we still expect
one more Fed hike to 4.00–4.25% and two more ECB hikes to 3.00% by year-end, with
cutting cycles starting only once inflation is back at target – earliest end-2027 – and

terminal rates of 3.50% (Fed) and 2.25% (ECB) reached in 2028.
• What it means for markets. We see bond yields dropping when markets start pricing
in our central bank scenario of a lower terminal rate in both Europe and the US.
The 10y Bund is expected to gradually drop from current levels to 3.3% at the end
of 2026 and further to 3.0% in 2028 (US 10y from 4.7% end of 2026 to 4.3% in 2028).
Credit spreads will slightly widen from current low levels given strong supply from

the technology sector. Equity markets will finish the year with double digits again but
decelerate to high single digits in 2027 and 2028, with valuations normalizing to historic
levels (S&P500 2026-2028: 14%, 8%, 8%, Eurostoxx: 15%, 9%, 8% total return). The euro
will gain gradually against the dollar, reaching 1.18 in 2028 as policy uncertainty and
a weaker economy in the US will weigh on the greenback. Private markets are entering
a more selective phase: Private equity faces a record ~13,300-company exit backlog,

### Page 5

28 September 2026
5
The summer turned three risks we had been
monitoring into part of our base case: a fresh energy
shock out of the Middle East, a bond sell-off that has
spread from the US to Japan to Europe and renewed
doubts about the AI boom. Yet the real economy has
not broken. Q2 growth surprised to the upside on net
exports, Germany‘s stimulus and robust AI-related
investment, and forward-looking indicators show limited
sign of deterioration so far, even as bond markets tell

a more troubled story, with yields at multi-year highs
and equities still reading the growth story rather than
the bond one. In our baseline scenario, global growth
slows from +3.0% in 2025 to +2.6% in 2026, recovers to
+2.8% in 2027 as the chokepoints fade and eases back to
+2.6% in 2028 as that support wanes. Within this, the US
moderates from +2.1% to +1.8%, the Eurozone recovers
from +1.0% to +1.3%, and Asia remains the main growth
engine throughout at around +4.4%. This path holds

only as long as the three chokepoints unwind rather
than escalate, and each rests on a specific, breakable
assumption. On energy, we assume the conflict stays
contained and that rerouting, demand destruction and
alternative supply let prices ease, contingent on the
Saudi pipeline outage not extending past a month; a
longer outage could push the shortfall above 8Mb/d and
Brent back near USD100/bbl, turning a contained shock
into a stagflationary one. On bonds, we assume inflation

moderates without a wage spiral and that sovereign
markets absorb elevated issuance without disorderly
repricing, a call that rests on European elections (France,
Italy) and the US midterms not adding fresh fiscal
uncertainty; if that gives way, a self-reinforcing yield-
fiscal loop could force central banks to choose between
fighting inflation and stabilizing bond markets. On
AI, we assume capex keeps supporting growth while
decelerating, peaking above USD1trn in 2028, contingent

on power and infrastructure bottlenecks staying
manageable and earnings continuing to validate the
spend; an investment retrenchment here would spill from
technology into corporate investment, consumption and
trade more broadly. Growth, inflation and rates in our
baseline hold only as long as these three assumptions
hold too.

It may get better if three assumptions hold

If our assumptions do not hold, we will stumble into

one or several downside scenarios ranging from
stagflation to demand-side deflationary recessions.
Table 2 sets out what happens if our assumptions
on chokepoints break. Should Middle East de-
escalation fail, the shock persists rather than fades:
oil stays above USD100/bbl into 2027 and the picture
turns stagflationary, with US and Eurozone growth
undershooting the baseline. The Eurozone would see
a recession, while inflation runs hotter and keeps rising

through 2027, forcing the Fed and ECB to hike further
to 5.00% and 4.00%, respectively. Long yields spike
first before reversing sharply lower once the growth
damage bites, and risk assets sell off hard through 2026
– equities barely positive, credit and sovereign spreads
wider, private equity, private debt and real estate all
negative – ahead of a partial rebound in 2027. An AI
bubble burst plays out in the opposite direction: the
shock is disinflationary, US growth stalls towards zero

by 2027 and the Fed and ECB slash rates to just 0.50%,
pulling long yields and oil sharply lower. Equities fall
hardest in the US, given its greater AI concentration,
while the damage is most acute in the areas most tied
to the capex cycle – private equity, private debt and,
notably, infrastructure, which turns negative for the only
time across the three scenarios. The third scenario is a
slower-moving chain reaction: a bond sell-off triggered
by a political shock – long yields spiking and OAT and

BTP spreads blowing out as fiscal risk resurfaces on both
sides of the Atlantic – triggers an equity correction, which
in turn causes a growth shock in the US and elsewhere
that eventually feeds through to sharply lower rates
and inflation, with the Fed cutting to 1.50% and the
ECB to 1.00% by 2027. Because the political shock hits
the US and Europe together, EURUSD stays essentially
unchanged from baseline throughout, even as growth

and rates swing sharply; peripheral European spreads,
however, stay persistently wider even once credit and
core yields have normalized, a lingering political risk
premium.

Global trade of goods in volume is expected to grow
at +4% y/y in 2026, driven mainly by AI, which masks
a more divergent global economy. AI-driven demand
will contribute 4.8pps to global trade, which together

with European defense spending (+0.2pp) and carryover
effects (+1.4pp) add further support. Without these
offsetting forces, the underlying picture would be
considerably weaker: The Strait of Hormuz closure is
estimated to subtract 2.1pps from trade growth, while
the ongoing trade war erases a further 0.4pp. The rest
of the global economy is contracting in trade terms, and
the +4% headline number conceals what is effectively a
two-speed world.
Global goods trade growth is volume is expected to

ease to +2.9% in both 2027 and 2028 as the forces
supporting the 2026 headline begin to fade.  AI-
driven demand will remain the largest contributor to
growth (+1pp), but at slower phase than prior years as
deceleration starts. Hormuz normalization is unlikely to
be linear, causing stock rebuilding to take longer and
extend to 2028. Defense spending remains a positive but
diminishing contributor. The trade war persists through
2027, with US tariffs continuing to disrupt established

supply chains, subtracting 0.3pp from growth. In parallel,
free-trade agreements (FTAs) signed over the past
12 months will begin generating measurable export
gains from 2027, adding around 0.1pp annually, with
beneficiaries concentrated in the EU, India, Mercosur, the
UAE and Australia.
At a country level, the clearest winners are economies
embedded in AI supply chains, concentrated in
Asia-Pacific, while structurally the US trade war
unleashes the so-called “China shock 2.0” amplifying

China’s presence through global supply chains .
South Korea and Taiwan are posting export growth
rates of +10.5% and +30% y/y, respectively, driven by
semiconductor exports and other AI inputs. Mexico,
Vietnam and Malaysia are benefiting from supply-chain
diversification, while Israel’s technology exports are
also on the rise. In the developed world, the US and the
Netherlands stand out. The structural consequence of US
tariffs, combined with China‘s weak domestic demand,

are amplifying China’s role in the global supply chain
under the „China shock 2.0.“ Unable to sell as much into

### Page 9

28 September 2026
9
the US, China is redirecting exports through the global
south and into Europe at record volumes, causing Europe
to be squeezed from both sides: more Chinese imports
competing with domestic industry, and fewer US export
opportunities due to tariffs.
The effective US tariff rate currently stands at
9.2% and is expected to reach 12% following
the implementation of the second Section 301
investigation on overcapacity in October.  Export losses

are estimated at USD42bn this year, after USD74bn in
2025. We expect tariffs on China at 32%, with potential
for lower rates on non-sensitive goods and fewer
restrictions as relations are likely to remain stable in the
near term. However, the trade war is likely to intensify
after the US midterm elections as a shift in Congressional
control would place constraints on the administration‘s
domestic policy agenda and push it to rely more heavily
on trade tools. On the downside, potential risks could

push the average US tariff rate up to 21.3%. The most
likely scenarios include higher-than-expected tariffs
under the overcapacity Section 301, the opening of
a new Section 301 investigation targeting countries
accused of transshipment on behalf of China, and
continued tensions with Canada following the escalation
over the summer. While we do not expect further
developments in the near term, the threat of 50% tariffs
on Canadian autos remains while USMCA renegotiations

progress very slowly. Finally, the EU should also remain
watchful, with two main tariff risks: one linked to the
global services tax and the other tied to the new US
sanctions on Russia, allowing for tariffs on Russian
energy importers.
The global fiscal outlook continues to deteriorate at
the margin as structural spending pressures – defense
foremost – combine with the renewed cost of energy-
related support measures.  Many governments were
initially reluctant to intervene, treating the current

shock as transitory; the repeated extension of these
„temporary“ measures has instead become a recurring
drag on consolidation efforts. Looking at the regions,
US fiscal policy is unlikely to turn significantly restrictive
amid a likely divided Congress (or a Democratic
sweep) after the November midterm elections. In
this environment, we would expect only limited fiscal
consolidation in 2027 and 2028, to the tune of 0.5%
of GDP in 2027 and 0.6% in 2028 of structural deficit

improvement, relying on a mix of discretionary and social
spending restraint and „stealth“ taxation increases. In
the Eurozone, political risk is an increasingly relevant
input for spread dynamics: With France, Italy and
Spain all facing elections in 2027, pre-electoral tax cuts
and spending pledges raise the risk of fiscal slippage
precisely as consolidation becomes more pressing.
Moreover, the phase-out of NGEU disbursements adds
a further headwind: As grants are booked as fiscal

revenue, their decline mechanically weighs on headline
deficits for the bloc‘s largest beneficiaries (Southern
European countries in particular), compounding the loss
of investment support at a time when fiscal buffers are
already thinning.

### Page 10

Allianz Research
10
Inflation rates will remain elevated until the first half
of 2027 as indirect effects of elevated input costs filter
through core consumer prices.  Sharply higher energy
prices in 2026 have quickly pushed up fuel inflation in
most economies, with gasoline prices reaching historical
highs. Meanwhile, utility bills have started to drift up,
especially in European countries, as higher wholesale
gas prices are gradually being passed through. In Q4

2026, we expect CPI headline inflation at +2.9% in Japan,
+3.3% in the Eurozone, +3.4% in the UK and +3.5% in the
US, with upwards risks. Higher utility bills should continue
through 2027 even if wholesale gas prices start to decline
as we expect. Persistently elevated energy and input
costs should also increasingly prompt corporates to
increase their selling prices to protect their margins. This
will filter through to consumer prices, mostly from end-

2026 and into 2027. In particular, consumer food prices
should accelerate significantly from early 2027. In most
developed markets, we expect headline inflation to start
pulling down decisively only from Q2 2027, thanks to
favorable base effects on fuel inflation, but core prices
will remain sticky. However, high inflation and tighter
financial conditions will weigh on growth, which should
eventually limit ‘second round’ effects of higher prices
onto wages and corporate profits, while lower oil and

gas prices will also help pull down fuel and core prices.
In this environment, we see inflation rates normalizing
from the end of 2027 and in 2028. We expect headline
CPI inflation to reach +2.1% by Q4 2027 in the Eurozone,
+2.2% in the UK, +2.5% in the US and +1.4% in Japan.
Inflation and central banks:
More hikes ahead before easing cycle starts
in Q4 2027

US and Eurozone labor markets are set to cool further
into 2027 as slowing hiring meets accelerating AI
adoption. In the US, August‘s steady headline (+162,000
payrolls, 4.1% unemployment) masks a thinning hiring
pipeline: Soft hiring intentions and cooling wage growth
point to further pullback ahead, even as layoffs stay
contained. In the Eurozone, we expect unemployment

to drift only marginally above current lows (6.4%)
as vacancies keep falling and wage growth keeps
normalizing. The adjustment should keep running
primarily through hiring pace rather than job cuts,
delaying, not removing, the risk of rising unemployment.
Sectoral divergence should also widen, with
manufacturing staying under pressure from weak demand
and high energy costs while services hold up better. AI
adoption adds a further, structural drag on hiring: Firms

are increasingly able to absorb demand growth without
adding headcount, and with productivity gains from AI
still limited so far, the near-term effect shows up more in
slower hiring than in higher output, reinforcing margin
pressure into 2027. Our base case remains a gradual,
not sharp, deterioration, with buffers thinner than a year
ago, and Europe‘s main vulnerability now industrial
rather than energy-driven.

We expect the Federal Reserve to deliver one final

hike in December, before maintaining a prolonged
hold for most of 2027. As we expected in our last
economic outlook, the Fed delivered a 25bps interest
rate hike at its September meeting. With price pressures
expected to remain elevated in the near term, we now
see the Fed delivering a follow up hike at its December
meeting. With economic activity and the labor market
remaining resilient, the Fed will stay vigilant about the
risks of second-round effects. However, this hiking cycle

should be limited to two rate hikes. For a start, the Fed
policy stance is much less loose than in 2021-23, with the
real Fed interest rate slightly positive today (vs largely
negative in 2021-2023). Second, corporate pricing power
is less strong. Back in 2021-23, high and rising margins
were widespread across sectors, pointing to an economy
running too hot. In contrast, the rise in profits today is
much more concentrated in a handful of sectors (ICT,

energy, electronics). Thanks to a soft economy, easing
supply-chain strains (including for AI) and lower expected
energy costs, inflation should eventually cool down,
prompting the beginning of a Fed normalization from
December 2027. We expect the Fed policy rate to settle
in the 3.25-3.5% bound by mid-2028.
Following the European Central Bank’s (ECB) second
rate hike this year in September to 2.50%, we expect
two further 25bps increases in October and December

2026, taking the deposit rate to 3.00%. The ECB’s
focus is likely to remain on containing the second-
round effects of the Middle-East-related energy shock,
particularly as resilient economic activity provides
room for further tightening. Thereafter, we expect a
prolonged pause through 2027. While inflation is likely
to remain elevated in the near term, our assumption
of gradually easing oil and gas prices should support
a renewed disinflationary trend, with inflation moving

back towards target during the second half of 2027.
Against this backdrop, we expect the ECB to resume
easing in 2028, delivering three 25bps cuts to 2.25%,

the upper end of the estimated neutral-rate range. This
would mark a gradual normalization of monetary policy

as energy-related pressures fade, while the ECB continues
to balance inflation risks against the potential drag from
higher borrowing costs on economic activity. Meanwhile,
quantitative tightening continues at its full run-off pace of
around EUR45bn per month and is expected to continue
for the foreseeable future as the stock of holdings is still
high. At the current pace it would still take six years to
run down the remaining EUR3.2trn of assets sitting in the

PEPP and APP program. The annualized balance-sheet
reduction equals around 3.3% of GDP and by that exceeds
the Eurozone’s fiscal deficit of around 3.1%, further
reinforcing the net supply pressure on government bonds.
The Bank of England (BoE) is expected to provide two
back-to-back rate hikes in November and December
as elevated core inflation prompt a response . The BoE
has refrained from hiking interest rates so far despite
persistently above-target inflation (even prior the Iran

crisis). Admittedly, the BoE’s policy rate is already elevated
at 3.75%, and there is scant evidence of accelerating core
inflation. However, the indirect effects of elevated input
costs will start to show up soon as corporates will start
to increase their selling prices. A surprisingly resilient
economy provides further impetus for a likely acceleration
of core inflation in the months ahead. The labor market is
loose, but the unemployment rate has stabilized around

5%. In this environment, we expect the BoE to turn more
hawkish and deliver back-to-back 25bps interest rate
hikes at its November and December meetings, pushing
the Bank Rate to 4.25%. We see a beginning of a
normalization in November 2027 as inflation pulls back
and the economy softens. We expect the Bank Rate to
reach 3% by Q3 2028.
The Bank of Japan (BoJ) delivered a 25bps hike to
1.25% in September, accelerating its tightening on the
back of resilient activity and firm wage growth despite

headwinds from the Middle East conflict.  Inflationary
pressures have remained contained, in part thanks to
government subsidies on energy, with headline inflation
near target. However, the pass-through from elevated
energy prices should push underlying momentum
higher in the near term, with inflation picking up in Q4
to bring 2026 to +2.1%. The yen‘s slide to 40-year lows
over the summer prompted intervention and reinforced
the faster tightening path. We expect the next hike in

January 2027 and one additional 25bps increase in H1
2027, reaching a terminal rate of 1.75%. With the Fed
expected to ease in 2028 and Prime Minister Takaichi set
to replace two hawkish board members in mid-2027, we
anticipate a first 25bps cut in late 2028.

Allianz Research
